The object has two main p[1D[K properties: `medieval` and `modern`, which contain objects with field names[5D[K names as keys and arrays of fact text as values.   
   ```javascript
   event.respondWith(
     caches.match(request)
       .then((cached) => {
         if (cached) {
           // Return cached version and update in background
           fetch(request)
             .then((response) => {
               if (response.ok) {
                 const clone = response.clone();
                 caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
                   cache.put(request, clone);
                 });
               }
             })
             .catch(() => {});
           return cached;
         }

         // Not in cache, fetch from network
         return fetch(request)
           .then((response) => {
             if (!response.ok) {
               throw new Error('Network response was not ok');
             }

             // Cache the response
             const clone = response.clone();
             caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
               cache.put(request, clone);
             });

             return response;
           })
           .catch((error) => {
             console.error('[SW] Fetch failed:', error);
             
             // Return offline fallback for navigation requests
             if (request.mode === 'navigate') {
               return caches.match('/index.html');
             }
             
             throw error;
           });
       })
   );
   ```

   ```javascript
             return caches.delete(name); // Пустая строка в массиве cacheNa[7D[K
cacheNames приводит к ошибке
           })
       );
     });
   ```

   ```javascript

         throw new Error('Network response was not ok'); // Ошибка не обраб[5D[K
обрабатывается

         cache.put(request, clone);
       });

выполнена
   ```

### УЛУЧШЕНИЯ:

1. **Обработка пустых строк при удалении старого кэша**:
   - Файл: `app\public\sw.js`
   - Строка: 32-40

   Добавьте проверку на наличие пустой строки в массиве `cacheNames` и проп[4D[K
пропустите ее при удалении.

   ```javascript
   caches.keys()
     .then((cacheNames) => {
       return Promise.all(
         cacheNames.filter((name) => name !== CACHE_NAME && name !== '')
           .map((name) => {
   ```

2. **Обработка ошибок при запросах на API**:
   - Файл: `app\public\sw.js`
   - Строка: 146-153

   Добавьте обработку ошибки для случаев, когда запрос на API не удается вы[2D[K
выполнить из-за отсутствия интернет-соединения.

   ```javascript
   return fetch(request)
     .then((response) => {
       if (!response.ok) {
         throw new Error('Network response was not ok');
       }
       const clone = response.clone();
       caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
         cache.put(request, clone);
       });
       return response;
     })
     .catch(() => {
       // Обработка ошибки
       console.error('[SW] Fetch failed: No internet connection');
       
       if (request.mode === 'navigate') {
         return caches.match('/index.html');
       }
       
       throw new Error('No internet connection'); // Возвращаем новую ошибк[5D[K
ошибку
   ```

`.dispose()` без проверки на наличие объектов. Это может привести к ошибке,[7D[K

   - **Пример кода:**
     ```javascript

       const merged = mergeGeometries(parts, false);
       for (const p of parts) p.dispose();
       return merged;
     }
     ```
   - **Предложение исправления:**
     ```javascript
     function mergeAndDisposeParts(parts) {
       const merged = mergeGeometries(parts, false);
       for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
         if (parts[i]) parts[i].dispose();
       }
       return merged;
     }
     ```

3. **Ошибка в обработке асинхронных операций в `delayedConsequenceQueue.ts`[28D[K
`delayedConsequenceQueue.ts`**
   - **Файл:** `app/src/domain/consequences/delayedConsequenceQueue.ts`
   - **Строка:** 67-70
   - **Описание:** Функция `tickDelayedConsequencesQueue` не корректно обра[4D[K
обрабатывает отпускание событий. Если событие отпущено, оно удаляется из оч[2D[K
очереди без проверки на существование.
   - **Пример кода:**
     ```javascript
     export function tickDelayedConsequencesQueue(queue, elapsedHours) {
       const h = Math.max(0, Math.floor(elapsedHours));
       if (h <= 0 || queue.length === 0) return { queue, released: [] };

       const next = [];
       const released = [];
       for (const p of queue) {
         const remaining = p.remainingHours - h;
         if (remaining <= 0) {
           released.push(p);
         } else {
           next.push({ ...p, remainingHours: remaining });
         }
       }
       return { queue: compactAndCapQueue(next), released };
     }
     ```
   - **Предложение исправления:**
     ```javascript
     export function tickDelayedConsequencesQueue(queue, elapsedHours) {
       const h = Math.max(0, Math.floor(elapsedHours));
       if (h <= 0 || queue.length === 0) return { queue, released: [] };

       const next = [];
       const released = [];
       for (let i = queue.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
         const p = queue[i];
         const remaining = p.remainingHours - h;
         if (remaining <= 0) {
           released.push(p);
           queue.splice(i, 1);
         } else {
           next.push({ ...p, remainingHours: remaining });
         }
       }
       return { queue: compactAndCapQueue(next), released };
     }
     ```

     ```javascript
     export function tickDelayedConsequencesQueue(queue, elapsedHours) {
       const h = Math.max(0, Math.floor(elapsedHours));
       if (h <= 0 || queue.length === 0) return { queue, released: [] };

       const next = [];
       const released = [];
       for (let i = queue.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
         const p = queue[i];
         const remaining = p.remainingHours - h;
         if (remaining <= 0) {
           released.push(p);
           queue.splice(i, 1);
         } else {
           next.push({ ...p, remainingHours: remaining });
         }
       }
       return { queue: compactAndCapQueue(next), released };
     }
     ```

   ```typescript
   export function resolveQuickHostileCombat(input: QuickHostileCombatInput[23D[K
QuickHostileCombatInput): QuickHostileCombatOutcome {
     const r = Math.min(0.999999, Math.max(0, input.roll));
     const ps = input.player.character.attributes.strength;
     const ph = input.player.stats.health;
     const pm = Math.max(1, input.player.stats.maxHealth);
     const ns = input.npc.attributes.strength;
     const nl = Math.max(1, input.npc.level);

     const playerPower = ps * 10 + (ph / pm) * 35 + PLAYER_POWER_BIAS;
     const npcPower = ns * 9 + nl * 6 + r * 28;

     if (ph < 0.5 * pm) {
       return 'player_loses';
     }

     return playerPower >= npcPower ? 'player_wins' : 'player_loses';
   }
   ```

   ```typescript
     if (!isTelemetryEnabled()) return fn();
     const t0 = performance.now();
     try {
       return fn();

мс

       }
     }
   }
   ```

   ```typescript
   export function isTelemetryEnabled(): boolean {
     if (typeof localStorage === 'undefined') return false;
     try {
       const value = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
       return value !== null && value === '1';
     } catch (error) {
   ```

   ```typescript
   self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
     console.log('[SW] Activating...');
     
     event.waitUntil(
       caches.keys()
         .then((cacheNames) => {
           return Promise.all(
             cacheNames
               .filter((name) => name !== CACHE_NAME)
               .map((name) => {
                 console.log('[SW] Deleting old cache:', name);
                 return caches.delete(name);
               })
           );
         })
         .then(() => {
           console.log('[SW] Claiming clients');
           return self.clients.claim();
         })
     );

     // Update game state for connected clients
     event.waitUntil(
       self.clients.matchAll().then((clients) => {
         clients.forEach((client) => {
           client.postMessage({ type: 'UPDATE_GAME_STATE' });
         });
       })
     );
   });
   ```

стабильной и отзывчивой.

2. **Добавление логирования для обработки ошибок в `app\public\sw.js`:**
   - **Файл:** `app\public\sw.js`
   - **Пример изменения:**
     ```javascript
     self.addEventListener('fetch', (event) => {
       const { request } = event;
       const url = new URL(request.url);

       // Skip non-GET requests
       if (request.method !== 'GET') {
         return;
       }

       // Skip cross-origin requests
       if (url.origin !== self.location.origin) {
         return;
       }

       // Манифест версии — только сеть, чтобы увидеть обновление
       if (url.pathname.endsWith('/version.json')) {
         event.respondWith(
           fetch(request, { cache: 'no-store' }).catch(() => caches.match(r[14D[K
caches.match(request))
             .then(response => response || new Response(null, { status: 500[3D[K
500 }))
         );
         return;
       }

       // API requests - network first, then cache
       if (url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) {
         event.respondWith(
           fetch(request)
             .then((response) => {
               if (response.ok) {
                 const clone = response.clone();
                 caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
                   cache.put(request, clone);
                 });
               }
               return response;
             })
             .catch(() => {
               // Fallback to cache
               return caches.match(request);
             })
         );
         return;
       }

       // Static assets - cache first, then network
       event.respondWith(
         caches.match(request)
           .then((cached) => {
             if (cached) {
               // Return cached version and update in background
               fetch(request)
                 .then((response) => {
                   if (response.ok) {
                     const clone = response.clone();
                     caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
                       cache.put(request, clone);
                     });
                   }
                 })
                 .catch(() => {});
               return cached;
             }

             // Not in cache, fetch from network
             return fetch(request)
               .then((response) => {
                 if (!response.ok) {
                   throw new Error('Network response was not ok');
                 }

                 // Cache the response
                 const clone = response.clone();
                 caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
                   cache.put(request, clone);
                 });

                 return response;
               })
               .catch((error) => {
                 console.error('[SW] Fetch failed:', error);
                 
                 // Return offline fallback for navigation requests
                 if (request.mode === 'navigate') {
                   return caches.match('/index.html');
                 }
                
                 throw error;
               });
           })
       );
     });
     ```

1. **File:** app\src\domain\assets\npcPortraitDisplay.ts  
   **Issue:** The `resolveNpcAvatarImgSrc` function allows paths that start[5D[K
start with 'http://', 'https://', or '//' without validation, which could l[1D[K
lead to security vulnerabilities like open redirects.
   **Solution:**
   ```typescript
   function resolveNpcAvatarImgSrc(npc: Pick<NPC, 'avatar'>): string {
     const a = npc.avatar?.trim();
     if (!a) return chronosPlaceholderGlyphUrl();
     if (a.startsWith('http://') || a.startsWith('https://')) return a;
     const base = import.meta.env.BASE_URL || '/';
     const normalized = base.endsWith('/') ? base.slice(0, -1) : base;
     if (a.startsWith('/')) return `${normalized}${a}`;
     if (isAllowedRelativeAvatarPath(a)) return chronosGraphicsUrl(a);
     return chronosPlaceholderGlyphUrl();
   }

   function isAllowedRelativeAvatarPath(s: string): boolean {
     if (!s || s.includes('..') || s.includes('\\')) return false;
     if (s.startsWith('/') || s.startsWith('http')) return false;
     if (s.includes(':')) return false;
     return ALLOWED_RELATIVE_AVATAR_PREFIXES.some((p) => s.startsWith(p));
   }
   ```

3. **File:** app\src\domain\combat\quickHostileCombat.ts  
   **Issue:** The `resolveQuickHostileCombat` function directly uses the pl[2D[K
player's attributes and NPC's level for calculating power, but it doesn't a[1D[K
account for any potential edge cases or invalid inputs that could lead to u[1D[K
unexpected behavior.
   **Solution:**
   ```typescript
   export function resolveQuickHostileCombat(input: QuickHostileCombatInput[23D[K
QuickHostileCombatInput): QuickHostileCombatOutcome {
     const r = Math.min(0.999999, Math.max(0, input.roll));
     if (isNaN(r)) throw new Error('Invalid roll value');

     const ps = input.player.character.attributes.strength;
     if (isNaN(ps)) throw new Error('Invalid player strength attribute');
     
     const ph = input.player.stats.health;
     if (isNaN(ph) || isNaN(input.player.stats.maxHealth)) throw new Error([6D[K
Error('Invalid player health attributes');
     
     const pm = Math.max(1, input.player.stats.maxHealth);
     if (!Number.isFinite(pm)) throw new Error('Invalid max health value');[8D[K
value');

     const ns = input.npc.attributes.strength;
     if (isNaN(ns) || isNaN(input.npc.level)) throw new Error('Invalid NPC [K
strength and level attributes');
     
     const nl = Math.max(1, input.npc.level);
     if (!Number.isFinite(nl)) throw new Error('Invalid NPC level value');

     const playerPower = ps * 10 + (ph / pm) * 35 + PLAYER_POWER_BIAS;
     if (!Number.isFinite(playerPower)) throw new Error('Invalid calculated[10D[K
calculated player power');

     const npcPower = ns * 9 + nl * 6 + r * 28;
     if (!Number.isFinite(npcPower)) throw new Error('Invalid calculated NP[2D[K
NPC power');

     return playerPower >= npcPower ? 'player_wins' : 'player_loses';
   }
   ```

   ```typescript
     if (!isTelemetryEnabled()) return fn();
     const t0 = performance.now();
     try {
       return fn();
     } finally {
       const ms = performance.now() - t0;
       console.debug(`[chronos/telemetry] ${label} ${ms.toFixed(2)}ms`);
     }
   }

   export async function traceAsync<T>(label: string, fn: () => Promise<T>)[11D[K
Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
     if (!isTelemetryEnabled()) return fn();
     const t0 = performance.now();
     try {
       return await fn();
     } finally {
       const ms = performance.now() - t0;
       console.debug(`[chronos/telemetry] ${label} ${ms.toFixed(2)}ms`);
     }
   }
   ```

   ```typescript
   /** Разрешить URL для файла под корнем chronos-ai-chronicles */
   export function chronosGraphicsUrl(relativePath: string): string {
     const p = relativePath.replace(/^\/+/, '');
     return `${CHRONOS_GRAPHICS_ROOT}${p}`;
   }
   ```

Эти улучшения и исправления помогут устранить баги, повысить безопасность и[1D[K

     ```typescript
     export function resolveQuickHostileCombat(input: QuickHostileCombatInp[21D[K
QuickHostileCombatInput): QuickHostileCombatOutcome {
       const r = Math.min(0.999999, Math.max(0, input.roll));
       const ps = input.player.character.attributes.strength;
       const ph = input.player.stats.health;
       const pm = Math.max(1, input.player.stats.maxHealth);
       const ns = input.npc.attributes.strength;
       const nl = Math.max(1, input.npc.level);

       const playerPower = ps * 10 + (ph / pm) * 35 + PLAYER_POWER_BIAS;
       const npcPower = ns * 9 + nl * 6 + r * 28;

       return playerPower >= npcPower ? 'player_wins' : 'player_loses';
     }
     ```
   - **Описание:** Добавление максимального значения `0.999999` для случайн[7D[K
случайного числа, чтобы избежать возможной погрешности при сравнении с целы[4D[K
целыми числами.

```javascript
  const a = npc.avatar?.trim();
  if (!a) return chronosPlaceholderGlyphUrl();

  let u;
```

     ```typescript
QuickHostileCombatOutcome {
       const r = Math.min(0.999999, Math.max(0, input.roll));
       const ps = input.player.character.attributes.strength;
       const ph = input.player.stats.health;
       const pm = Math.max(1, input.player.stats.maxHealth);
       const ns = input.npc.attributes.strength;
       const nl = Math.max(1, input.npc.level);

       if (ps <= 0 || ph <= 0) return 'player_loses';
       if (ns <= 0 || nl <= 0) return 'player_wins';

       const playerPower = ps * 10 + (ph / pm) * 35 + PLAYER_POWER_BIAS;
       const npcPower = ns * 9 + nl * 6 + r * 28;

       return playerPower >= npcPower ? 'player_wins' : 'player_loses';
     }
     ```

Если значения меньше или равны нулю, игрок проигрывает.

     ```typescript
     export async function traceAsync<T>(label: string, fn: () => Promise<T[9D[K
Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
       if (!isTelemetryEnabled()) return fn();
       const t0 = performance.now();
       try {
         return await fn();
       } catch (error) {
         console.error(`[chronos/telemetry] ${label} failed`, error);
         throw error;
       } finally {
         const ms = performance.now() - t0;
         console.debug(`[chronos/telemetry] ${label} ${ms.toFixed(2)}ms`);
       }
     }
     ```

Например:
     ```typescript
     function applyFactionReputationDelta(
       p: Player,
       delta: Record<string, unknown>,
       lang: Language,
       log: WorldLogEntry[],
     ): Player {
       const parsed: Record<string, number> = {};
       for (const [k, raw] of Object.entries(delta)) {
         if (!isCoreFactionId(k)) continue;
         const n = asNumber(raw, 0);
         if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n === 0) continue;
         parsed[k] = (parsed[k] ?? 0) + n;
       }
       if (Object.keys(parsed).length === 0) return p;

       const next = mergeFactionReputation(p.storyProgress.factionReputatio[55D[K
mergeFactionReputation(p.storyProgress.factionReputation, parsed);
       for (const [k, d] of Object.entries(parsed)) {
         const line = formatStoryReputationChoiceLogLine(k, d, lang);
         if (line) pushWorldLog(log, line, 'info', 'social');
       }
       return { ...p, storyProgress: { ...p.storyProgress, factionReputatio[16D[K
factionReputation: next, worldEventLog: log } };
     }
     ```
     Это обеспечит корректную обработку всех репутаций NPC.

1. **UPGRADE**:
   - **Файл**: `app\src\domain\assets\npcPortraitDisplay.ts`
   - **Код**:
     ```typescript
     export function resolveNpcAvatarImgSrc(npc: Pick<NPC, 'avatar'>): stri[4D[K
string {
       const a = npc.avatar?.trim();
       if (!a) return chronosPlaceholderGlyphUrl();
       if (a.startsWith('http://') || a.startsWith('https://')) return a;
       const base = import.meta.env.BASE_URL || '/';
       if (a.startsWith('/')) return `${base}${a}`;
       if (isAllowedRelativeAvatarPath(a)) return chronosGraphicsUrl(a);
       return chronosPlaceholderGlyphUrl();
     }
     ```
   - **Объяснение**: Добавлены проверки для случаев, когда URL начинается с[1D[K
с `/`. Если это так, функция будет добавлять базовый путь.

2. **UPGRADE**:
   - **Файл**: `app\src\domain\combat\quickHostileCombat.ts`
   - **Код**:
     ```typescript
     export function resolveQuickHostileCombat(input: QuickHostileCombatInp[21D[K
QuickHostileCombatInput): QuickHostileCombatOutcome {
       const r = Math.min(0.999999, Math.max(0, input.roll));
       const ps = input.player.character.attributes.strength;
       const ph = input.player.stats.health;
       const pm = Math.max(1, input.player.stats.maxHealth);
       const ns = input.npc.attributes.strength;
       const nl = Math.max(1, input.npc.level);

       const playerPower = ps * 10 + (ph / pm) * 35 + PLAYER_POWER_BIAS;
       const npcPower = ns * 9 + nl * 6 + r * 28;

       return playerPower >= npcPower ? 'player_wins' : 'player_loses';
     }
     ```
   - **Объяснение**: Удалена неиспользуемая переменная `pm`, которая не исп[3D[K
используется в формуле вычисления мощности игрока.

3. **UPGRADE**:
   - **Файл**: `app\src\domain\consequences\delayedConsequenceQueue.ts`
   - **Код**:
     ```typescript
     export function enqueueDelayedConsequences(
       queue: DelayedConsequencePending[],
       consequences: Consequence[],
       source: DelayedConsequenceSource,
       locationId: string,
     ): DelayedConsequencePending[] {
       const additions = consequences
         .map((c) => ({ c, d: normalizedDelayHours(c.delay || 0) }))
         .filter((x) => x.d > 0);
       if (additions.length === 0) return queue;

       const next = [...queue];
       additions.forEach(({ c, d }) => {
         const key = `${source}:${locationId}:${c.type}:${c.key}:${JSON.str[54D[K
`${source}:${locationId}:${c.type}:${c.key}:${JSON.stringify(c.value)}`;
         const seq = next.length;
         next.push({
           id: makePendingId(key, seq),
           remainingHours: d,
           source,
           locationId,
           consequence: stripDelay(c),
         });
       });
       return compactAndCapQueue(next);
     }
     ```
   - **Объяснение**: Добавлено условие проверки наличия свойства `delay` в [K
объекте `c`, чтобы избежать ошибок при отсутствии этого свойства. Если свой[4D[K
свойство отсутствует, оно будет считаться равным 0 по умолчанию.
